We can also use a more sophisticated direction: solve least squares to reduce weighted active coefficients under L2 norm constraint. The linearized change in K_t ≈ 2⟨P_t x, d⟩. With weights w, minimize ∑ w_t 2⟨P_t x, d⟩ = 2⟨G, d⟩ subject to ∑d=0, ||d||≤1. Solution d = - (G - mean G)/||G-mean||. Same as steepest descent in ℓ2. Good. We can use that, step = alpha/n. This is what we do: d = -g_centered / norm? If norm includes scale. We can simply `d = -gc`; `d = d - d.mean`; `norm = np.linalg.norm(d)`; `d = d/norm`; update `x_new = x + step*d` where step=alpha/n maybe. Let's detail subgradient run for given n:
```
def run_subgradient(n, seed, duration, target_score=None):
   rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
   # init
   x = generate_initial(n, rng)
   x = normalize(x)
   best_x = x.copy(); best_score = score_internal(x)
   v = np.zeros(n)
   beta = 0.5
   no_improve = 0
   tau_alpha = 1.0 # decay
   step_alphas = [0.05,0.1,0.2,0.5,1.0,2.0,4.0]
   last_time = time.monotonic()
   it=0
   while time.monotonic() < last + duration and it < max_iters:
       c, M = conv_max(x)
       score=2*n*M
       if score < best_score: ...
       # if target reached maybe reduce? no
       # weights
       T = max(M*0.1, 1e-30)
       w = np.exp((c - M)/T)
       wsum=w.sum()
       if wsum<=0: w= active center
       else w/=wsum
       # gradient
       G = conv_first_n(x, w) * 2.0
       G -= G.mean()
       # momentum
       v = 0.5*v + G
       v -= v.mean()
       d = -v  # descent
       # normalize direction max=1
       mn = np.max(np.abs(d))
       if mn <= 1e-30: perturb
       d = d/mn
       best_local_x = x
       best_local_score = score
       for alpha in step_alphas:
           step = alpha / n
           xn = x + step*d
           xn = np.clip(xn, 0, None)
           sx = xn.sum()
           if sx <= 0: continue
           xn /= sx
           s_new = score_internal_fast? We already have c? Need recompute. For each alpha, compute conv_max, costly 7 evals. Maybe too much.
           if s_new < best_local_score: take
       x = best_local_x
       if best_local_score < best_score - 1e-15: no_improve=0 else no_improve++
       if no_improve > 500: randomize/perturb, reset v, maybe change step_alphas larger
       # if stagnation severe: cross? 
   return best_x,best_score
```

`run_subgradient(n, seed, duration, threshold_start, threshold_end, use_provided=False)` returns best_x, best_score_internal, c? We need exact candidates.

Implementation details in run:
```
def run_subgradient(n, seed, duration, provided_seq=None):
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    s_c = fast_len(2*n-1)
    s_g = fast_len(3*n-2)
    use_direct = n <= DIRECT_LIMIT
    # initial candidates to evaluate and choose best start
    starts=[]
    for kind in range(8): x=make_initial(...); s=score_internal(x,...); starts.append(...)
    if provided_seq is not None: x=normalize(provided_seq); s=...
    choose best start
    x = start
    c = conv_full_auto(x, s_c, use_direct)
    v = np.zeros(n)
    beta = 0.3
    alpha = 0.2
    threshold = 0.6
    no_improve = 0
    current_score = 2*n*max(c)
    best_score = current_score
    best_x = x.copy()
    start_time = time.monotonic()
    max_duration = duration
    iter=0
    while time.monotonic()-start_time < max_duration:
        iter += 1
        M = float(np.max(c))
        if M <= 1e-300: break
        current_score = 2*n*M
        if current_score < best_score:
            best_score = current_score; best_x = x.copy(); best_update_time=time
        # schedule: threshold from 0.6 to 0.95 over duration? maybe use iter
        progress = min(1.0, (time.monotonic()-start_time)/max_duration)
        threshold = 0.5 + 0.45*(1 - math.exp(-progress*3))? start broad 0.5, later 0.95
        # maybe anneal temp acceptance
        w = active_weights(c,M, threshold)
        g = gradient_conv(a,w,s_g,use_direct)
        g -= np.mean(g)
        v = beta*v + g
        v -= np.mean(v)
        mn = float(np.max(np.abs(v)))
        if not np.isfinite(mn) or mn < 1e-30:
            # random step
            d = rng.standard_normal(n); d -= d.mean(); mn=...; v=...
        d = -v / mn
        step = alpha / n
        # maintain positive
        xn = x + step*d
        # clip
        np.maximum(xn, 0.0, out=xn)
        sx = float(xn.sum())
        if sx <= 0:
            xn = x.copy(); c_new=c; score_new=current_score
        else:
            xn *= 1.0/sx
            c_new = conv_full_auto(xn, s_c, use_direct)
            score_new = 2*n*float(np.max(c_new))
        accept=False
        if score_new < current_score:
            accept=True
            no_improve=0
            alpha = min(3.0, alpha*1.05)
        else:
            no_improve += 1
            # annealing accept
            if no_improve > 20:
                temp = 1e-5 * math.exp(-no_improve/500) ?
                diff = score_new - current_score
                if diff < 0.05 and rng.random() < math.exp(-diff/max(temp,1e-12)):
                    accept=True
            if not accept:
                alpha *= 0.8
                if alpha < 0.02:
                    alpha=0.2
        if accept:
            x=xn; c=c_new; current_score=score_new
            # beta adjust? if improved often beta=0.3, else?
        else:
            # keep velocity? maybe decay velocity to avoid stale
            v *= 0.8
        # periodic floor and perturbation
        if no_improve > 300:
            # multiplicative perturbation
            sigma = 0.1 + 0.3*rng.random()
            xmin = float(np.mean(x))
            x = x * np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, sigma, n))
            # floor
            floor = 1e-8 * xmin
            x = np.maximum(x, floor)
            s = x.sum()
            x *= 1.0/s
            v = np.zeros(n)
            alpha = max(0.1, min(1.0, alpha))
            c = conv_full_auto(x, s_c, use_direct)
            current_score = 2*n*float(np.max(c))
            no_improve = 0
            # maybe threshold=0.5
            if no_improve? 
        # if very bad no improvement for 1500 and best unchanged, restart with new initial
        if no_improve > 1500 and time_left>2:
            x = make_initial(n,rng,int(rng.integers(0,5)))
            v=zeros; c=conv; current=score; no_improve=0
        # if best_score <= 1.01 target? could break? No need.
    return best_x, best_score
```

Let's define `search_n(n, budget_s, seed, top_candidates, provided_seq=None)`:
- if budget <=2 return.
- Set s_c, s_g, use_direct.
- Determine number of subgradient runs: `runs = max(1, min(8, int(budget_s/25)+1))`. Each duration = budget_s/runs. But leave 20% for local refinement of best.
- For each run:
   - initial: if run==0 and provided_seq length n: use provided; else make_initial with kind=run%8, sigma random.
   - call run_subgradient with duration = run_duration*0.8
   - if time remains and run is best for n, call local_refine on best_x for maybe 20s.
   - Update best_n and top_candidates.
- After all, if best_n internal maybe exact? Not now.

def choose_initial(n, rng, provided=None):
   cands=[]
   for mode in range(6): cands.append(make_initial(...))
   if provided: cands.append(normalize(provided))
   evaluate all with score_internal; return best.
```

- 4: linear with random slope plus quadratic

Let's write top-level `make_initial(n, rng, mode)`:
```
def make_initial(n, rng, mode):
  if mode == 0: a=np.ones(n); a*=np.exp(rng.normal(0,0.05,n));
  elif mode==1: a=np.exp(rng.normal(0,0.5,n))
  elif mode==2: t=np.linspace(0,1,n); beta=float(rng.uniform(0,2)); a=np.exp(beta*t) (or 1,t^p)
  elif mode==3: t=...; m=int(rng.integers(1,6)); phase=rng.uniform(0,2π); a=1+0.7*np.cos(2π*m*t+phase); plus noise
  elif mode==4: t=...; a=np.abs(np.sin(np.pi*(t**p)))? 
  else: blocks.
  a = np.maximum(a,1e-8); normalize
```
Avoid using `np.linspace`? okay.

`upsample_sequence(a_small, n)`: if len not equal. Could repeat with `np.interp`:
```
def upsample_sequence(a, n):
  m=len(a); if m==n return a; xs_old=np.arange(m); xs_new=np.linspace(0,m-1,n); return np.maximum(np.interp(xs_new,xs_old,a),0)
```
But need not if choose initial by mode.

def local_refine(x, n, s_c, use_direct, duration, seed):
  rng=default_rng(seed)
  x=copy? current=x.copy()
  c=conv_full_auto(x,s_c,use_direct); current_score=2n*max(c); best_score=current; best_x=x
  width_frac = ...
  while time < end:
    move = rng.integers(0,3)
    if move==0: block multiplicative
       width = int(n * rng.uniform(0.001,0.05)) +1
       start = rng.integers(0,n)
       stop=min(n,start+width)
       cand=x.copy(); cand[start:stop]*=np.exp(rng.normal(0, sigma, width)); normalize
       sigma rng 0.05-0.5
    elif move==1: coordinate transfer
       i,j sampled; delta=alpha*mean; direction random; cand=x.copy(); cand[i]+=delta; cand[j]-=delta; clip; normalize
       alpha 0.05-1
    else: random smooth: cand=x*np.exp(rng.normal(0,0.025,n)); normalize
    c_new=conv_full_auto(cand,s_c,use_direct); score_new=...
    if score_new < current_score: accept
    else: maybe anneal accept if close?;
    if best improve store
  return best_x,best_score
```

`DIRECT_LIMIT = 300`

`normalize_vector(x)`:
```
def normalize_vector(x):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64)
    x = np.maximum(x, 0.0)
    s = float(np.sum(x))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return np.full(x.shape, 1.0/x.size)
    x = x / s
    # clamp max 1000 after normalize (not needed)
    return x
```

`make_initial(n, rng, mode)`: top-level. Need ensure mode might be provided sequence? We'll handle separately.
```
def make_initial(n, rng, mode):
  if mode == 0: a=np.ones(n); a *= np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.05, n))
  elif mode == 1: a=np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.4, n))
  elif mode == 2:
     t=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
     beta=float(rng.uniform(-2.0,2.0))
     a=np.exp(beta*t)
  elif mode == 3:
     t=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
     m=int(rng.integers(1,6))
     phase=float(rng.uniform(0.0,2.0*math.pi))
     amp=float(rng.uniform(0.3,0.8))
     a=1.0 + amp*np.cos(2.0*math.pi*m*t + phase)
     a += 0.05*rng.standard_normal(n)
  elif mode == 4:
     t=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
     p=float(rng.uniform(0.3,3.0))
     a=(t**p)+(1.0-t)**p + 1e-3
  else:
     a=np.ones(n)
     k=int(rng.integers(1,4))
     for _ in range(k):
        l=int(rng.integers(1,n))
        c=int(rng.integers(0,n))
        stop=min(n,c+l)
        if stop>c:
           a[c:stop] *= float(rng.uniform(0.2,3.0))
  a=np.maximum(a,1e-8)
  return normalize_vector(a)
```
Top-level loop in mode 5 okay.

Active weights function:
```
def active_weights(c, M, threshold):
   if M <= 0:
      w=np.zeros(c.shape); idx=int(np.argmax(c)); w[idx]=1; return w
   ratio = c / M
   w = ratio - threshold
   np.maximum(w, 0.0, out=w)
   total = float(np.sum(w))
   if total <= 1e-300:
      w.fill(0.0); idx=int(np.argmax(c)); w[idx]=1.0
   else:
      w /= total
   return w
```
`w` new array each iter. Could reuse? no.

Gradient conv:
```
def conv_first(a,w,s_len,use_direct):
  n=a.size
  if use_direct:
     g=np.convolve(a,w)[:n]
  else:
     Fa=np.fft.rfft(a,s_len)
     Fw=np.fft.rfft(w,s_len)
     g=np.fft.irfft(Fa*Fw,s_len)[:n]
  np.maximum(g,0.0,out=g)
  return g
```
Full conv:
```
def conv_full(x,s_len,use_direct):
  if use_direct:
    c=np.convolve(x,x)
  else:
    F=np.fft.rfft(x,s_len)
    c=np.fft.irfft(F*F,s_len)[:2*x.size-1]
  np.maximum(c,0.0,out=c)
  return c
```
But if using `np.fft.irfft(F*F, s_len)` returns length s_len; if F*F has rounding imaginary? okay. `np.maximum` with out=c works if c contiguous. If direct c length 2n-1. Good.

In run loop, we can compute `g = conv_first(x,w,s_grad,use_direct)`; but for n large, Fa computed again. We also computed c via conv_full maybe separate FFT. Could combine to save if use_direct false: We can compute Fa once and both c and g, but c already available from previous iteration. When x changes, we need new c for candidate. For current x, c stored. For gradient we need Fa current; c already stored. Could compute Fa from current x and g, no need recompute c. That saves candidate score? Wait c stored from accepted candidate; to ensure consistency, c corresponds to x. We can recompute Fa for gradient and use c. If not accept previous, x unchanged, c unchanged, but we still compute Fa each iter. Could we precompute and reuse if no accept? We compute Fa each iter because needed. c stored. Candidate `x_new` requires new score; we can compute conv_full for candidate separately. So per iter gradient uses one FFT of x, candidate score uses one FFT of x_new. We avoid c FFT. Good. However active weights need c from previous. Good.

Loop code detailed:
```
    while time.monotonic() < end_time:
        M = float(np.max(c))
        current_score = 2.0 * n * M
        if current_score < best_score:
            best_score = current_score
            best_x = x.copy()
        # threshold schedule
        frac = min(1.0, (time.monotonic() - start_time)/duration)
        threshold = 0.6 + 0.35 * frac  # 0.6 to 0.95
        # maybe if no_improve high, broaden
        if no_improve > 100:
            threshold = min(threshold, 0.5)
        w = active_weights(c, M, threshold)
        g = conv_first(x, w, s_grad, use_direct)
        mean_g = float(np.mean(g))
        g -= mean_g
        v = beta * v + g
        v -= float(np.mean(v))
        mn = float(np.max(np.abs(v)))
        if not np.isfinite(mn) or mn <= 1e-300:
            d = rng.standard_normal(n)
            d -= float(np.mean(d))
            mn = float(np.max(np.abs(d)))
            v = d
        d = -v / mn
        step = alpha / n
        # maybe skip if step too large cause all clip; alpha adapt
        cand = x + step * d
        np.maximum(cand, 0.0, out=cand)
        sx = float(np.sum(cand))
        if sx <= 1e-300:
            alpha *= 0.5
            no_improve += 1
            continue
        cand *= 1.0 / sx
        c_new = conv_full_auto(cand, n, s_len, use_direct)
        score_new = 2.0 * n * float(np.max(c_new))
        accepted = False
        if score_new < current_score:
            accepted = True
            no_improve = 0
            if alpha < 2.0:
                alpha *= 1.03
            beta = 0.35
        else:
            no_improve += 1
            alpha *= 0.85
            if alpha < 0.05:
                alpha = 0.25
                v *= 0.5
            # optionally accept small worsen
            if no_improve > 50:
                diff = score_new - current_score
                temp = 1e-4 / (1.0 + no_improve)
                if diff < 0.1 and rng.random() < math.exp(-diff / temp):
                    accepted = True
                    no_improve = 10
        if accepted:
            x = cand
            c = c_new
            current_score = score_new
        else:
            v *= 0.8
        # floor to keep positive? maybe every 100
        if no_improve > 250:
            sigma = float(rng.uniform(0.05,0.4))
            x = x * np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, sigma, n))
            x = np.maximum(x, 1e-10)
            x = normalize_vector(x)
            v = np.zeros(n)
            alpha = float(rng.uniform(0.1,0.6))
            c = conv_full_auto(x,n,s_len,use_direct)
            current_score = 2.0*n*float(np.max(c))
            no_improve = 0
            if current_score < best_score: ...
        if no_improve > 1200:
            # full restart
            mode = int(rng.integers(0,6))
            x = make_initial(n,rng,mode)
            v = np.zeros(n)
            alpha = 0.25
            c = conv_full_auto(x,n,s_len,use_direct)
            current_score = 2.0*n*float(np.max(c))
            no_improve=0
            beta=0.3
    return best_x, best_score

def local_refine(x,n,duration,seed,s_len,use_direct):
  x=x.copy(); rng=default_rng(seed); end=time+duration
  c=conv_full_auto; current_score=...; best...
  no_improve=0
  while time<end:
    r=rng.random()
    cand=x.copy()
    if r<0.4:
      width=int(1 + rng.integers(0, max(1,n//200)))? but range too small. Use width = int(n*rng.uniform(0.001,0.05))+1
      start=int(rng.integers(0,n))
      stop=min(n,start+width)
      sigma=float(rng.uniform(0.05,0.5))
      cand[start:stop] *= np.exp(rng.normal(0.0,sigma,width))
    elif r<0.7:
      i=int(rng.integers(0,n)); j=int(rng.integers(0,n))
      if i==j: continue
      mean=1.0/n
      delta = mean * float(rng.uniform(0.05,1.0)) * (1.0 if rng.random()<0.5 else -1.0)
      cand[i]+=delta; cand[j]-=delta
      np.maximum(cand,0,out=cand)
    else:
      sigma=float(rng.uniform(0.01,0.15))
      cand *= np.exp(rng.normal(0.0,sigma,n))
      np.maximum(cand,0,out=cand)
    sx=float(cand.sum())
    if sx<=0: continue
    cand*=1/sx
    cnew=conv_full_auto(cand,n,s_len,use_direct)
    snap=2*n*max(cnew)
    if snap < current_score:
       accept
    elif no_improve>20 and snap-current_score<1e-4:
       accept minor? maybe no.
    if no_improve>200: global smooth perturbation? 
  return best_x,best_score
```

def search_n(n, budget, seed, top_candidates, provided_by_len):
   if budget <= 3: return

```
def upsample_sequence(a, n):
  a=np.asarray(a,float)
  m=a.size
  if m==n: return a
  if m<2: return np.full(n,a[0] if m else 1.0)
  old=np.arange(m); new=np.linspace(0.0,m-1.0,n); return np.maximum(np.interp(new,old,a),0.0)
```

def top_candidates_add(top, seq, n, score):
    seq_list = [float(v) for v in seq]
    item = (float(score), int(n), len(top), seq_list)
    top.append(item)
    if len(top) > 100:
        top.sort()
        del top[50:]
```

Let's detailed code for `run_subgradient`:
```
def run_subgradient(n, seed, duration, provided_arr, s_len, s_grad, use_direct):
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    x = choose_initial(n, rng, provided_arr, s_len, use_direct)
    # perturb if near uniform
    uniform = 1.0 / n
    if np.max(np.abs(x - uniform)) < 1e-12:
       x = normalize_vector(np.maximum(x + rng.normal(0.0,0.01,n),0.0))
    v = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    c = conv_full_auto(x,n,s_len,use_direct)
    current_score = 2.0*n*float(np.max(c)); current_M = current_score/(2.0*n)
    best_score=current_score; best_x=x.copy()
    if duration <=0: return best_x,best_score
    start_time = time.monotonic(); end_time=start_time+max(0.0,duration)
    alpha=0.25; beta=0.35; no_improve=0; floor=1e-10
    while time.monotonic() < end_time:
       M=current_M
       if M <= 1e-300: break
       # threshold
       frac = (time.monotonic()-start_time)/duration
       if frac >1: frac=1
       threshold = 0.60 + 0.35*frac
       if no_improve > 150: threshold = min(threshold, 0.50)
       w = active_weights(c, M, threshold)
       g = conv_first_auto(x,w,s_grad,use_direct)
       g -= float(np.mean(g))
       v = beta*v + g
       v -= float(np.mean(v))
       mn = float(np.max(np.abs(v)))
       if not np.isfinite(mn) or mn <= 1e-300:
          d = rng.standard_normal(n)
          d -= float(np.mean(d)); mn=float(np.max(np.abs(d))); v=d
       d = -v / mn
       step = alpha / float(n)
       cand = x + step*d
       np.maximum(cand, 0.0, out=cand)
       # add tiny floor? could change objective; do occasionally? skip in main to preserve.
       sx = float(np.sum(cand))
       if sx <= 1e-300:
          alpha *= 0.5; no_improve +=1; continue
       cand *= 1.0/sx
       c_new = conv_full_auto(cand,n,s_len,use_direct)
       score_new = 2.0*n*float(np.max(c_new))
       accept=False
       if score_new < current_score:
          accept=True; no_improve=0
          if alpha < 2.0: alpha *= 1.03
          # beta maybe
       else:
          no_improve +=1
          alpha *= 0.85
          if alpha < 0.05:
             alpha = 0.25; v *= 0.5
          if no_improve > 60:
             diff = score_new - current_score
             temp = 1e-4 / (1.0 + 0.01*no_improve)
             if diff < 0.1 and rng.random() < math.exp(-diff/temp):
                accept=True; no_improve=20
       if accept:
          x = cand; c = c_new; current_score = score_new; current_M = score_new/(2.0*n)
          if current_score < best_score:
             best_score = current_score; best_x = x.copy()
       else:
          v *= 0.8
       if no_improve > 250:
          sigma = float(rng.uniform(0.05,0.35))
          x = x * np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, sigma, n))
          x = np.maximum(x, floor)
          x = normalize_vector(x)
          v = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
          alpha = float(rng.uniform(0.1,0.5))
          c = conv_full_auto(x,n,s_len,use_direct)
          current_score = 2.0*n*float(np.max(c)); current_M=current_score/(2.0*n)
          no_improve = 0
          if current_score < best_score: ...
       if no_improve > 1500:
          x = make_initial(n, rng, int(rng.integers(0,6)))
          v = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
          alpha = 0.25; no_improve=0
          c = conv_full_auto(x,n,s_len,use_direct)
          current_score = 2.0*n*float(np.max(c)); current_M=current_score/(2.0*n)
    return best_x,best_score
```

```
def select_final(top, fallback, time_budget_s):
   end = time.monotonic() + max(5.0, float(time_budget_s))
   fb_list = [float(v) for v in fallback]
   best_score = exact_score_from_list(fb_list)
   best_seq = fb_list if np.isfinite(best_score) else fb_list
   if not np.isfinite(best_score): best_score = 2.0
   if len(top)==0: return best_seq,best_score
   top_sorted=top[:]; top_sorted.sort()
   count=0
   for item in top_sorted:
      if time.monotonic() >= end - 2.0: break
      score,n,idx,seq = item
      count +=1
      if n <= 2048 and count > 60: break
      if n > 2048 and count > 20: break
      # skip if internal score >= best_score + 0.05? Since exact could be lower? If internal much worse, unlikely. But due rounding maybe. Use if score > best_score + 0.05: since sorted ascending, we can break? If sorted by internal scores. If current internal > best_score+0.05, subsequent internal worse, exact maybe not lower by >0.05? Could but unlikely. Break for time. But if best_score 2 and internal 2.01, exact maybe 1.5? impossible if internal accurate? FFT error not 0.5. Break. Use 0.01? To be safe, don't break, only if count limits.
      val = exact_score_from_list(seq)
      if np.isfinite(val) and val < best_score:
         best_score=val; best_seq=seq
   return best_seq,best_score
```

def sanitize_list(seq):
    arr=np.asarray(seq,float).copy()
    arr=np.clip(arr,0.0,1000.0)
    arr=np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
    s=float(np.sum(arr))
    if s <= 1e-12:
       arr=np.full(1024,1.0/1024.0)
       return [float(v) for v in arr]
    arr = arr / s
    arr = np.maximum(arr, 1e-12)
    arr = arr / arr.sum()
    # ensure max <1000
    return [float(v) for v in arr]
```

    try:

```python
"""
Search for non-negative coefficients minimizing

    2 * n * max(convolve(a, a)) / sum(a)^2

using a combination of:
  * FFT-based internal scoring,
  * active-set projected subgradient descent on the simplex,
  * targeted pair-transfer perturbations on active convolution sums,
  * block/random local refinement,
  * multiple lengths and random restarts.

The final answer is verified with the provided exact evaluator whenever
possible, with a safe uniform fallback.
"""

import time
import math
import numpy as np

DIRECT_LIMIT = 256


def normalize_vector(x):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if x.size == 0:
        x = np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    x = np.where(np.isfinite(x), x, 0.0)
    x = np.maximum(x, 0.0)
    s = float(np.sum(x))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return np.full(x.size, 1.0 / max(1, x.size), dtype=np.float64)
    return x / s


def fast_len(m):
    m = int(max(2, m))
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()


def conv_full_auto(x, n, s_len, use_direct):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64)
    if use_direct and n <= DIRECT_LIMIT:
        c = np.convolve(x, x)
    else:
        f = np.fft.rfft(x, s_len)
        c = np.fft.irfft(f * f, s_len)[:2 * n - 1]
    np.maximum(c, 0.0, out=c)
    return c


def score_internal_auto(x, n, s_len, use_direct):
    if n <= 0:
        return float("inf")
    c = conv_full_auto(x, n, s_len, use_direct)
    m = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(m):
        return float("inf")
    return 2.0 * float(n) * m


def conv_first_auto(a, w, s_len, use_direct):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    w = np.asarray(w, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    if use_direct and n <= DIRECT_LIMIT:
        g = np.convolve(a, w)[:n]
    else:
        fa = np.fft.rfft(a, s_len)
        fw = np.fft.rfft(w, s_len)
        g = np.fft.irfft(fa * fw, s_len)[:n]
    np.maximum(g, 0.0, out=g)
    return g


def active_weights(c, m, threshold):
    if m <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(m):
        w = np.zeros(c.shape, dtype=np.float64)
        if w.size:
            w[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
        return w
    w = c / m - threshold
    np.maximum(w, 0.0, out=w)
    s = float(np.sum(w))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        w.fill(0.0)
        if w.size:
            w[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
    else:
        w /= s
    return w


def make_initial(n, rng, mode):
    n = int(n)
    if mode == 0:
        a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        a *= np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.05, n))
    elif mode == 1:
        a = np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.45, n)).astype(np.float64)
    elif mode == 2:
        t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
        beta = float(rng.uniform(-2.0, 2.0))
        a = np.exp(beta * t).astype(np.float64)
    elif mode == 3:
        t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
        m = int(rng.integers(1, 6))
        phase = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * math.pi))
        amp = float(rng.uniform(0.25, 0.85))
        a = 1.0 + amp * np.cos(2.0 * math.pi * m * t + phase)
        a += 0.04 * rng.standard_normal(n)
    elif mode == 4:
        t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
        p = float(rng.uniform(0.35, 3.2))
        a = np.power(t + 1e-3, p) + np.power(1.0 - t + 1e-3, p) + 1e-3
    elif mode == 5:
        a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        k = int(rng.integers(1, 5))
        for _ in range(k):
            l = int(rng.integers(1, max(1, n)))
            c = int(rng.integers(0, max(1, n)))
            stop = min(n, c + l)
            if stop > c:
                a[c:stop] *= float(rng.uniform(0.2, 3.0))
    else:
        a = np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.7, n)).astype(np.float64)
    a = np.maximum(a, 1e-8)
    return normalize_vector(a)


def upsample_sequence(a, n):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = int(n)
    m = int(a.size)
    if m == n:
        return a
    if m == 0:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    if m == 1:
        return np.full(n, float(a[0]), dtype=np.float64)
    old = np.arange(m, dtype=np.float64)
    new = np.linspace(0.0, m - 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
    return np.maximum(np.interp(new, old, a), 0.0)


def get_provided_array():
    try:
        seq = height_sequence_1
    except Exception:
        return None
    try:
        arr = np.asarray(seq, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    except Exception:
        return None
    if arr.size == 0:
        return None
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(arr)):
        arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
    arr = np.maximum(arr, 0.0)
    if np.sum(arr) <= 0.0:
        return None
    return arr


def choose_initial(n, rng, provided_arr, s_len, use_direct):
    cands = []
    for mode in range(6):
        cands.append(make_initial(n, rng, mode))
    if provided_arr is not None:
        if provided_arr.size == n:
            cands.append(normalize_vector(provided_arr.copy()))
        elif provided_arr.size >= 2:
            cands.append(normalize_vector(upsample_sequence(provided_arr, n)))
    best = None
    best_score = float("inf")
    for a in cands:
        s = score_internal_auto(a, n, s_len, use_direct)
        if np.isfinite(s) and s < best_score:
            best_score = s
            best = a
    if best is None:
        best = np.full(n, 1.0 / n, dtype=np.float64)
    return best


def run_subgradient(n, seed, duration, provided_arr, s_len, s_grad, use_direct):
    n = int(n)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    duration = max(0.0, float(duration))

    x = choose_initial(n, rng, provided_arr, s_len, use_direct)
    if n > 1:
        uniform = 1.0 / float(n)
        if float(np.max(np.abs(x - uniform))) < 1e-12:
            x = normalize_vector(np.maximum(x + rng.normal(0.0, 0.01, n), 0.0))

    c = conv_full_auto(x, n, s_len, use_direct)
    current_m = float(np.max(c))
    current_score = 2.0 * n * current_m
    best_score = current_score
    best_x = x.copy()

    if duration <= 0.0:
        return best_x, best_score

    v = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    alpha = 0.25
    beta = 0.35
    no_improve = 0
    start_time = time.monotonic()
    end_time = start_time + duration

    while time.monotonic() < end_time:
        if not np.isfinite(current_m) or current_m <= 1e-300:
            break

        frac = (time.monotonic() - start_time) / duration
        if frac < 0.0:
            frac = 0.0
        if frac > 1.0:
            frac = 1.0
        threshold = 0.60 + 0.35 * frac
        if no_improve > 150:
            threshold = min(threshold, 0.50)

        w = active_weights(c, current_m, threshold)
        g = conv_first_auto(x, w, s_grad, use_direct)
        g -= float(np.mean(g))

        v = beta * v + g
        v -= float(np.mean(v))
        mn = float(np.max(np.abs(v)))

        if not np.isfinite(mn) or mn <= 1e-300:
            d = rng.standard_normal(n)
            d -= float(np.mean(d))
            mn = float(np.max(np.abs(d)))
            v = d

        d = -v / mn
        step = alpha / float(n)
        cand = x + step * d
        np.maximum(cand, 0.0, out=cand)
        sx = float(np.sum(cand))

        if sx <= 1e-300:
            alpha *= 0.5
            no_improve += 1
            continue
        cand *= 1.0 / sx

        c_new = conv_full_auto(cand, n, s_len, use_direct)
        m_new = float(np.max(c_new))
        score_new = 2.0 * n * m_new

        accept = False
        if score_new < current_score:
            accept = True
            no_improve = 0
            if alpha < 2.0:
                alpha *= 1.03
        else:
            no_improve += 1
            alpha *= 0.85
            if alpha < 0.05:
                alpha = 0.25
                v *= 0.5
            if no_improve > 60:
                diff = score_new - current_score
                temp = 1e-4 / (1.0 + 0.01 * float(no_improve))
                if diff < 0.1 and rng.random() < math.exp(-diff / temp):
                    accept = True
                    no_improve = 20

        if accept:
            x = cand
            c = c_new
            current_m = m_new
            current_score = score_new
            if current_score < best_score:
                best_score = current_score
                best_x = x.copy()
        else:
            v *= 0.8

        if no_improve > 250:
            sigma = float(rng.uniform(0.05, 0.35))
            x = x * np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, sigma, n))
            x = np.maximum(x, 1e-10)
            x = normalize_vector(x)
            v = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
            alpha = float(rng.uniform(0.1, 0.5))
            c = conv_full_auto(x, n, s_len, use_direct)
            current_m = float(np.max(c))
            current_score = 2.0 * n * current_m
            no_improve = 0
            if current_score < best_score:
                best_score = current_score
                best_x = x.copy()

        if no_improve > 1500:
            x = make_initial(n, rng, int(rng.integers(0, 6)))
            v = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
            alpha = 0.25
            no_improve = 0
            c = conv_full_auto(x, n, s_len, use_direct)
            current_m = float(np.max(c))
            current_score = 2.0 * n * current_m
            if current_score < best_score:
                best_score = current_score
                best_x = x.copy()

    return best_x, best_score


def local_refine(x, n, duration, seed, s_len, use_direct):
    n = int(n)
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).ravel().copy()
    x = normalize_vector(x)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    duration = max(0.0, float(duration))
    if duration <= 0.0:
        c = conv_full_auto(x, n, s_len, use_direct)
        return x, 2.0 * n * float(np.max(c))

    c = conv_full_auto(x, n, s_len, use_direct)
    current_score = 2.0 * n * float(np.max(c))
    best_score = current_score
    best_x = x.copy()

    start_time = time.monotonic()
    end_time = start_time + duration
    no_improve = 0

    while time.monotonic() < end_time:
        r = rng.random()
        cand = x.copy()
        m = current_score / (2.0 * float(n))

        if r < 0.25:
            # Block multiplicative perturbation.
            width = int(max(1, n * rng.uniform(0.001, 0.05)))
            start = int(rng.integers(0, max(1, n)))
            stop = min(n, start + width)
            if stop > start:
                sigma = float(rng.uniform(0.05, 0.45))
                cand[start:stop] *= np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, sigma, width - start))

        elif r < 0.45:
            # Random coordinate transfer.
            i = int(rng.integers(0, n))
            j = int(rng.integers(0, n))
            if i != j:
                delta = (1.0 / n) * float(rng.uniform(0.05, 1.0))
                if rng.random() < 0.5:
                    cand[i] += delta
                    cand[j] -= delta
                else:
                    cand[j] += delta
                    cand[i] -= delta

        elif r < 0.85:
            # Pair transfer on an active convolution sum.
            t_idx = int(rng.integers(0, c.size))
            if m <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(m) or c[t_idx] < 0.7 * m:
                t_idx = int(np.argmax(c))
            i = int(rng.integers(0, n))
            j = t_idx - i
            if 0 <= j < n and i != j:
                xi = float(cand[i])
                xj = float(cand[j])
                smaller = min(xi, xj)
                if smaller > 0.0:
                    amt = smaller * float(rng.uniform(0.05, 0.5))
                    if xi > xj:
                        cand[i] += amt
                        cand[j] -= amt
                    else:
                        cand[j] += amt
                        cand[i] -= amt

        else:
            # Small global smooth perturbation.
            sigma = float(rng.uniform(0.01, 0.15))
            cand *= np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, sigma, n))

        np.maximum(cand, 0.0, out=cand)
        sx = float(np.sum(cand))
        if sx <= 1e-300:
            continue
        cand *= 1.0 / sx

        c_new = conv_full_auto(cand, n, s_len, use_direct)
        score_new = 2.0 * n * float(np.max(c_new))

        accept = False
        if score_new < current_score:
            accept = True
            no_improve = 0
        else:
            no_improve += 1
            if no_improve > 50:
                diff = score_new - current_score
                if diff < 1e-4 and rng.random() < 0.20:
                    accept = True

        if accept:
            x = cand
            c = c_new
            current_score = score_new
            if current_score < best_score:
                best_score = current_score
                best_x = x.copy()

        if no_improve > 200:
            sigma = float(rng.uniform(0.05, 0.30))
            x = x * np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, sigma, n))
            x = np.maximum(x, 1e-10)
            x = normalize_vector(x)
            c = conv_full_auto(x, n, s_len, use_direct)
            current_score = 2.0 * n * float(np.max(c))
            no_improve = 0
            if current_score < best_score:
                best_score = current_score
                best_x = x.copy()

    return best_x, best_score


def top_candidates_add(top, seq, n, score):
    if seq is None:
        return
    score = float(score)
    if not np.isfinite(score):
        return
    seq_list = [float(v) for v in np.asarray(seq, dtype=np.float64)]
    item = (score, int(n), len(top), seq_list)
    top.append(item)
    if len(top) > 100:
        top.sort()
        del top[50:]


def search_n(n, budget, seed, top_candidates, provided_arr):
    n = int(n)
    budget = max(0.0, float(budget))
    if budget < 4.0 or n <= 0:
        return

    s_len = fast_len(2 * n - 1)
    s_grad = fast_len(3 * n - 2)
    use_direct = (n <= DIRECT_LIMIT)

    start_time = time.monotonic()
    end_time = start_time + budget
    if end_time <= start_time:
        return

    sub_budget = 0.85 * budget
    refine_budget = 0.15 * budget

    if n >= 4096:
        runs = 1
    elif n >= 1024:
        runs = 2
    else:
        runs = 3

    best_x = None
    best_score = float("inf")
    run_budget = sub_budget / float(max(1, runs))

    for r in range(runs):
        remaining = end_time - time.monotonic()
        if remaining < 4.0:
            break
        bt = min(run_budget, remaining - max(1.0, 0.25 * refine_budget))
        if bt < 3.0:
            break
        x, score = run_subgradient(
            n,
            seed + 101 * r,
            bt,
            provided_arr,
            s_len,
            s_grad,
            use_direct,
        )
        top_candidates_add(top_candidates, x, n, score)
        if np.isfinite(score) and score < best_score:
            best_score = score
            best_x = x

    if best_x is not None:
        remaining = end_time - time.monotonic()
        rb = min(refine_budget, remaining - 2.0)
        if rb > 3.0:
            xr, sr = local_refine(
                best_x,
                n,
                rb,
                seed + 202,
                s_len,
                use_direct,
            )
            top_candidates_add(top_candidates, xr, n, sr)
            if np.isfinite(sr) and sr < best_score:
                best_score = sr
                best_x = xr


def exact_score_from_list(seq):
    try:
        val = evaluate_sequence(seq)
        val = float(val)
    except Exception:
        val = float("inf")
    return val


def sanitize_final_list(seq):
    arr = np.asarray(seq, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if arr.size == 0 or not np.any(np.isfinite(arr)):
        arr = np.full(1024, 1.0 / 1024.0, dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
        arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
        s = float(np.sum(arr))
        if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-12:
            arr = np.full(1024, 1.0 / 1024.0, dtype=np.float64)
        else:
            arr = arr / s
    arr = np.maximum(arr, 1e-12)
    arr = arr / float(np.sum(arr))
    arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
    return [float(v) for v in arr]


def choose_final(top_candidates, fallback, time_budget_s):
    end_time = time.monotonic() + max(5.0, float(time_budget_s))
    fb = sanitize_final_list(fallback)
    best_score = exact_score_from_list(fb)
    best_seq = fb
    if not np.isfinite(best_score):
        best_score = 2.0

    if not top_candidates:
        return best_seq, best_score

    ordered = top_candidates[:]
    ordered.sort()
    count = 0
    for item in ordered:
        if time.monotonic() >= end_time - 2.0:
            break
        score, n, _, seq = item
        count += 1
        if n <= 2048 and count > 70:
            break
        if n > 2048 and count > 20:
            break
        val = exact_score_from_list(seq)
        if np.isfinite(val) and val < best_score:
            best_score = val
            best_seq = seq
    return best_seq, best_score


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400.0, **kwargs):
    for key in ("budget_s", "budget", "time_limit", "time_limit_s", "time"):
        if key in kwargs:
            try:
                budget_s = float(kwargs[key])
                break
            except Exception:
                pass
    if "seed" in kwargs:
        try:
            seed = int(kwargs["seed"])
        except Exception:
            pass

    total_budget = max(30.0, float(budget_s))
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    reserve = min(200.0, max(20.0, total_budget * 0.09))
    search_time = max(30.0, total_budget - reserve)

    top = []
    fallback = [1.0 / 1024.0] * 1024

    provided_arr = get_provided_array()
    if provided_arr is not None:
        s_len = fast_len(2 * provided_arr.size - 1)
        prov_score = score_internal_auto(
            normalize_vector(provided_arr),
            provided_arr.size,
            s_len,
            False,
        )
        top_candidates_add(top, normalize_vector(provided_arr), provided_arr.size, prov_score)

    top_candidates_add(top, fallback, 1024, 2.0)

    base = [
        (64, 10),
        (128, 20),
        (256, 40),
        (512, 80),
        (1024, 200),
        (2048, 500),
        (4096, 400),
        (8192, 300),
        (16384, 170),
    ]
    total_base = float(sum(b for _, b in base))
    scale = search_time / total_base if total_base > 0 else 1.0

    for n, base_budget in base:
        b = base_budget * scale
        remaining = search_time - (time.monotonic() - t0)
        if b < 4.0 or remaining < 10.0:
            continue
        b = min(b, remaining - 5.0)
        if b < 4.0:
            continue
        print(f"searching n={n} budget={b:.1f}s", flush=True)
        search_n(n, b, seed + n * 17, top, provided_arr)
        if top:
            top.sort()
            print(f"  best internal so far: {top[0][0]:.12f} at n={top[0][1]}", flush=True)

    remaining_for_exact = total_budget - (time.monotonic() - t0) - 2.0
    remaining_for_exact = min(200.0, max(5.0, remaining_for_exact))
    print("selecting exact best candidate", flush=True)
    best_seq, best_score = choose_final(top, fallback, remaining_for_exact)
    best_seq = sanitize_final_list(best_seq)
    print(f"final internal/exact selected score: {best_score:.12f}, length={len(best_seq)}", flush=True)
    return best_seq
```
